```
// 后期三大 Boss AI 1:1 移植(Terarria1456 = 1.4.5.6 反编译源码,经典模式主链):
//   aiStyle 84  拜月教邪教徒 439  AI_084_LunaticCultist()        NPC.cs:64921-65957
//   aiStyle 120 光之女皇     636  AI_120_HallowBoss()           NPC.cs:46299-47302(+DashTo :47304)
//   aiStyle 121 史莱姆皇后   657  AI_121_QueenSlime()           NPC.cs:45543-46177(+FlyMovement :45467)
// 由 Enemy.fixedUpdate 的 aiStyle 分发调用(接线见文件尾「接线清单」);本文件不改动任何已有文件。
// 弹幕统一走 bossAI.shoot(Arrow 直飞桶);原版「定点预告→延时冲刺」类投射物(465/919/923)
// 收敛为带初速直飞(见各处注释)。getGoodWorld 分支已实装(女皇招式间隔折半/史后空中
// 横移+砸地蓄力+凝胶环抬档);expert/大师分支只注释不实现;netcode 跳过。
import { Enemy } from './Enemy';
import type { GameHooks } from './types';
import type { Player } from './Player';
import type { Camera } from '../render/Camera';
import type { Renderer } from '../render/Renderer';
import { TILE } from '../core/constants';
import { TILE_DEFS } from '../data/tiles';
import { spawnPart, shoot, losClear, addEnemy } from './bossAI';
import { Entity } from './Entity';
import { projSprite } from './Arrow';

// ===================== 共用工具(与 bossAI.ts 同语义,私有副本) =====================

/** 原版 MathHelper.Clamp */
const clamp = (v: number, lo: number, hi: number): number => (v < lo ? lo : v > hi ? hi : v);
/** 原版 Utils.GetLerpValue(clamped) */
const lerpValue = (from: number, to: number, t: number): number =>
  from === to ? (t < from ? 0 : 1) : clamp((t - from) / (to - from), 0, 1);
/** 原版 Vector2.Lerp */
const vLerp = (a: number, b: number, t: number): number => a + (b - a) * t;

/** 伤害/防御基值缓存(状态机每 tick 从基值派生临时修正,防重复乘膨胀;同 bossAI.baseStats) */
const baseStats = new WeakMap<Enemy, { dmg: number; def: number }>();
const baseOf = (e: Enemy): { dmg: number; def: number } => {
  let b = baseStats.get(e);
  if (!b) {
    // 首次进入时 def 可能已被别的状态改写——重新取原版数据基值兜底
    b = { dmg: e.def.damage, def: e.def.defense ?? 0 };
    if (e.vanilla) {
      b.dmg = e.vanilla.damage ?? b.dmg;
      b.def = e.vanilla.defense ?? b.def;
    }
    baseStats.set(e, b);
  }
  return b;
};

/** 原版 localAI[0..3] 的外部存储(Enemy 只有 ai0-ai3 四个槽)。
 *  la2/la3 放宽为 unknown:邪教徒用 la2 存仪式圈心坐标对象、la3 存克隆体的真身引用 */
const localAI = new WeakMap<Enemy, { la0: number; la1: number; la2: unknown; la3: unknown }>();
const laOf = (e: Enemy): { la0: number; la1: number; la2: unknown; la3: unknown } => {
  let l = localAI.get(e);
  if (!l) { l = { la0: 0, la1: 0, la2: 0, la3: 0 }; localAI.set(e, l); }
  return l;
};

/** 原版 SimpleFlyMovement(NPC.cs:23866 雏形):逐轴向目标速度以加速度步进(钳 ±acc) */
function simpleFly(e: Enemy, dvx: number, dvy: number, acc: number): void {
  e.vx += clamp(dvx - e.vx, -acc, acc);
  e.vy += clamp(dvy - e.vy, -acc, acc);
}
```

```
	private void AI_120_HallowBoss_DashTo(Vector2 targetPosition)
	{
		DirectionTo(targetPosition);
		targetPosition += new Vector2(0f, -300f);
		if (Distance(targetPosition) > 200f)
		{
			targetPosition -= DirectionTo(targetPosition) * 100f;
		}
		Vector2 vector = targetPosition - base.Center;
		float lerpValue = Utils.GetLerpValue(100f, 600f, vector.Length(), clamped: true);
		float num = vector.Length();
		if (num > 18f)
		{
			num = 18f;
		}
		velocity = Vector2.Lerp(vector.SafeNormalize(Vector2.Zero) * num, vector / 6f, lerpValue);
	}

	public bool Boss_CanShootExtraAt(int playerIndex, int rotationIndexToAttack, int rotationSize, float attackScanDistance, bool alwaysSkipMainTarget = true)
	{
		if (playerIndex == target && alwaysSkipMainTarget)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (playerIndex % rotationSize != rotationIndexToAttack)
		{
			return false;
		}
		Player player = Main.player[playerIndex];
		if (!player.active || player.dead || !playerInteraction[playerIndex])
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (Distance(player.Center) > attackScanDistance)
		{
			return false;
		}
		return true;
	}
```

```
Full Duke reference AI captured. Summary of Duke:

**Constants:**
- num = expertMode ? 1.2 : 1

- num2 = flag3 ? 3 : 5 (charges count — phase2: 3, phase1: 5? Actually num2 = flag3 ? 3 : 5; flag5 = ai[3] < num2*2 → "early charges" (speedup)
- Damage/defense: flag4 → damage = defDamage*1.1*num, defense=0; flag3 → damage=defDamage*1.2*num, defense=defDefense*0.8; else base.
- num3 (hover duration): expert 40 / normal 60; flag4: 30; flag3&&flag5: expert 40/normal 20; flag5&&!flag3&&!flag4: 30.
- num4 (accel): expert 0.55/normal 0.45; flag4: 0.7; flag3&&flag5: expert 0.6/normal 0.5; 
- num5 (hover speed): expert 8.5/normal 7.5; flag4: 12; flag3&&flag5: expert 10/normal 8.
- num6 (charge duration): expert 28/normal 30; flag4: 25; flag5&&flag3: expert 27/normal 30.
- num7 (charge speed): expert 17/normal 16; flag4: 27; flag5&&flag3 expert: 21.
- num8=80 (bubble phase dur), num9=4 (bubble interval), num10=0.3 (bubble hover accel), num11=5 (bubble hover speed), num12=90 (sharknado windup), num13=180 (phase2 transition), num14=180 (phase3 transition), num15=30 (phase3 teleport cycle), num16=120 (spiral duration), num17=4 (spiral bubble interval), num18=6 (spiral bubble speed), num19=20 (phase2 attack entry speed), num20=2π/(num16/2)=2π/60 (spiral rotation/tick), num21=75 (spawn delay).

- Target: player.dead or dist > 5600 → fly up vy-=0.4, EncourageDespawn(10), ai[0]>4→5 else 0, ai[2]=0.
- flag6 = ENRAGE: player.y<800 (space) or y > worldSurface*16 (below surface) or X in 6400..maxTilesX*16-6400 (not ocean sides). Enraged: num3=10, damage=defDamage*2, defense=defDefense*2, num7+=6.
- localAI[0]==0 init: ai[0]=-1 (spawn state), alpha=255.
- Rotation: angle toward player + π if spriteDirection==1; states -1,3,4,8 → target rotation 0; rotation speed num23: 0.04 base; 0 for states 1,6,7; 0.01 for 3,4,8.
- Alpha: states !=-1 && <9: solid collision → +15 else -15, clamp 0..150 (transparent when not colliding = 150 alpha = invisible? in Terraria alpha 255=opaque; 0=fully visible? Actually alpha in Terraria: 0 = fully opaque drawn, 255 = invisible. So alpha up to 150 = semi-transparent while moving through open water; solid collision → visible (alpha→0).
- State -1 (spawn): velocity*=0.98, face player; ai[2]>20: vy=-2, alpha fades; ai[2]==num12-30=60: 36 dust ring + PlaySound(29, style 20); ai[2]>=75 → state 0.
- State 0 (phase1 hover): hover toward (player + (ai[1], -200)) where ai[1]=300*sign(away); accel num4 toward speed num5 with direction-crossing double-accel; ai[2]>=num3 → attack select by ai[3]: cases 0-9 → num28=1 (charge); 10 → ai[3]=1, num28=2 (bubbles); 11 → ai[3]=0, num28=3 (sharknado); enraged && num28==2 → 3; flag (≤50% HP) → num28=4 (phase transition). 
  - case1 charge: velocity = normalize(player-center)*num7 (16/17), state 1.
  - case2: state 2 (bubbles).
  - case3: state 3 (sharknado), enraged → ai[2]=num12-40 (50).

- State 2 (bubbles): hover accel num10=0.3 toward speed num11=5; at ai[2]==0: PlaySound(29,...,20); every num9=4 ticks: PlaySound(4,...,19) + NewNPC 371 (ExplodingBubble) at direction*(width+20)/2 toward player, +45 y. Duration num8=80 → state 0.
- State 3 (sharknado windup): velocity*=0.98, vy lerp 0.02; at ai[2]==num12-30=60: PlaySound(29,...,9) + spawn 2 SharknadoBolt 385 at rotation*(UnitX*direction)*(width+20)/2+center with vx ±direction*2, vy=8; duration 90 → state 0.
- State 4 (phase2 transition): flag7=false (invuln), velocity*=0.98 vy lerp; at ai[2]==num13-60=120: PlaySound(29,...,20); at 180 → state 5, ai[3]=0.
- State 5 (phase2 hover): like state 0 but attacks: ai[3] 0-5 → 1 (charge); 6 → ai[3]=1, 2 (bubble ring, enters with velocity=normalize*20 num19, state 7); 7 → ai[3]=0, 3 (sharknado big → state 8); flag2 (expert ≤15%) → 4 (state 9, phase3 transition); enraged&&2→3.
```

```
632	function empressDashTo(e: Enemy, tx0: number, ty0: number): void {
633	  let tx = tx0, ty = ty0 - 300;
634	  const d0 = Math.hypot(tx - e.cx, ty - e.cy);
635	  if (d0 > 200) {
636	    const ux = (tx - e.cx) / (d0 || 1), uy = (ty - e.cy) / (d0 || 1);
637	    tx -= ux * 100; ty -= uy * 100;
638	  }
639	  const vx = tx - e.cx, vy = ty - e.cy;
640	  const len = Math.hypot(vx, vy) || 1;
641	  const lv = lerpValue(100, 600, len);
642	  const cap = Math.min(len, 18);
643	  e.vx = vLerp((vx / len) * cap, vx / 6, lv);
644	  e.vy = vLerp((vy / len) * cap, vy / 6, lv);
645	}

648	function empressHover(e: Enemy, player: Player, ox: number, oy: number, spdMul = 1, accMul = 1): void {
649	  const tx = player.cx + ox, ty = player.cy + oy;
650	  if (Math.hypot(tx - e.cx, ty - e.cy) > 40) {
651	    const dx = tx - e.cx, dy = ty - e.cy;
652	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
653	    simpleFly(e, (dx / d) * 12 * spdMul, (dy / d) * 12 * spdMul, 0.5 * accMul);
654	  }
655	}

657	export function empressOfLightAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null): void {
658	  if (!e.bInit) {
659	    e.bInit = true;
660	    e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0;
661	    e.spawnAlpha = 254; // Opacity = ai1/180 渐显(:46410)
662	    game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy - 80, '#FFE080', 20, 2.5);
663	  }
664	  const phase2 = e.ai3 === 1 || e.ai3 === 3; // AI_120_HallowBoss_IsInPhase2 :46179
665	  const dayEnrage = game.world.clock.isDay;  // flag4 = ShouldEmpressBeEnraged() = Main.dayTime（:46296）
666	  // flag2 = Main.expertMode（:46317）；白天暴怒 flag4 时强制置真（:46355）——
667	  // 全六处行为：num17+5（:46364）/选招起手环移（:46603）/case2 射速 2（:46632）/
668	  // case4 五枪+450 环（:46711-46731）/case6 八辐（:46859）/case7 六线 18 枪（:46888）
669	  const flag2 = game.world.isExpert || dayEnrage;
670	  // num17：二阶段 +15，flag2 再 +5（:46360-46367）——各态时长均减 num17
671	  const num17 = (phase2 ? 15 : 0) + (flag2 ? 5 : 0);
672	  const b = baseOf(e);
673	  // :47296-47301 二阶段防御 ×1.2;白天暴怒（flag4,:47296 段 damage=9999——wiki"白天必秒杀"）
674	  // ——2026-08-13 接通:白天召唤女皇不再无风险
675	  // :46319-46323 满血+白天且未暴怒 → ai3 += 2（白天召出即 Enraged 档 2;
676	  // 受过伤再转白天不补——原版"只在白天受过伤"语义）。NpcDrops 的
677	  // EmpressOfLightIsGenuinelyEnraged(:46169)读 ai3∈{2,3} → 夜光 5005 掉落门
678	  if (e.hp === e.maxHp && dayEnrage && e.ai3 !== 2 && e.ai3 !== 3) e.ai3 += 2;
679	  e.def.damage = dayEnrage ? 9999 : b.dmg;
680	  e.def.defense = Math.floor(phase2 ? b.def * 1.2 : b.def);
681	
682	  // 目标失效/超 6400px → 淡出退场(:46437-46444/:46595 num36=13;此后只有 13 态不触 player)
683	  // flag12（:46584-46594）：真狂暴（ai3∈{2,3}）且（入夜 或 白天 Main.time≥53400——
684	  // 昼 54000t 的末 600t ≈ 19:20PM 后）→ 强制 13 态离场。Main.time 换算：
685	  // (timeOfDay − DAWN) × 86400（昼 0.625 天 ↔ 54000t）
686	  const genuinelyEnraged = e.ai3 === 2 || e.ai3 === 3;
687	  const mainTime = (game.world.clock.timeOfDay - 4.5 / 24) * 86400;
688	  const enragedLeave = genuinelyEnraged && (!dayEnrage || mainTime >= 53400);
689	  if ((!player || player.dead || Math.hypot(player.cx - e.cx, player.cy - e.cy) > 6400 || enragedLeave) && e.ai0 !== 13) {
690	    e.ai0 = 13; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 += 1;
691	    e.vx /= 4; e.vy /= 4;
692	  }
693	  if (player) e.facing = e.seekDirX(player);
694	  if (!player) {
695	    // 目标彻底失效:上方已强制 ai0=13,此处推进淡出自毁(:47236/:47264-47271)
696	    e.spawnAlpha = Math.min(254, e.spawnAlpha + 5);
697	    e.vx *= 0.95; e.vy *= 0.95;
698	    e.ai1 += 1;
699	    flyIntegrate(e);
700	    if (e.ai1 >= 20 && e.spawnAlpha >= 254) e.dead = true;
701	    return;
702	  }
703	
704	  // 冲刺窗口伤害倍率(num16,:46352/:47006):8/9 态 40-90t 时 ×1.5

710	  e.def.damage = dayEnrage ? 9999 : Math.round(b.dmg * (dashBoost ? 1.5 : 1));
711	
712	  switch (e.ai0) {
713	    // ---- 0 出生(:46370-46422):velocity=(0,5) 后 0.95 阻尼,180t 渐显;全程无敌(flag6=false) ----
714	    case 0: {
715	      if (e.ai1 === 0) {
716	        e.vx = 0; e.vy = 5;
717	        // :46376 出生头顶 874 极光演出弹（0 速 0 伤）
718	        shoot(game, e.cx, e.cy - 80, 0, 0, 0, 874);

720	      if (e.ai1 === 10) game.playSfxWav?.('Item_161', 1, e.cx, e.cy);   // :46381

722	      e.vx *= 0.95; e.vy *= 0.95;
```

```
1064	function queenFlyMovement(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player): void {
1065	  e.onGround = false;
1066	  const spd = 12, accBase = 0.085, hover = 250;
1067	  let tx = player.cx, ty = player.cy - hover;
1068	  if (losClear(game, e.cx, e.cy, player.cx, player.cy)) {
1069	    tx = player.cx; ty = player.cy - hover;

1092	  else if (dist < 80) { dvx = (dvx / (dist || 1)) * spd * 0.65; dvy = (dvy / (dist || 1)) * spd * 0.65; }
1093	  else { dvx = (dvx / (dist || 1)) * spd; dvy = (dvy / (dist || 1)) * spd; }
1094	  simpleFly(e, dvx, dvy, acc);
1095	  e.spin = clamp(e.vx * 0.1, -0.5, 0.5); // :45536-45541
1096	}
```

```
85	interface HeadX { f2: number; f3: number; l0: number; l1: number; l2: number }
86	const headX = new WeakMap<Enemy, HeadX>();
87	const headOf = (e: Enemy): HeadX => {
88	  let x = headX.get(e);
89	  if (!x) { x = { f2: 0, f3: 0, l0: 0, l1: 0, l2: 0 }; headX.set(e, x); }
90	  return x;
91	};
92	
93	/** 实心块内判定(原版 Collision.SolidCollision 的中心点近似) */
94	const inSolid = (game: GameHooks, e: Enemy): boolean => {
95	  const st = game.world.store;
96	  const tx = Math.floor(e.cx / TILE), ty = Math.floor(e.cy / TILE);
97	  return st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.isSolid(tx, ty);
98	};
99	
100	/** 原版"攻击时间表"推进(NPC.cs :37229-37251 / :37740-37762 / :38243-38264 同构):
101	 *  ai1 全局计时,逐槽累计时长定位当前槽;全部槽走完回绕 ai1=0。
102	 *  注意:只回写 ai1,不写 ai0——手/头由调用方把槽攻击号赋给 ai0(即其状态),
103	 *  核心二阶段 ai0 是相位状态(0/1/2/3),绝不能被攻击号覆盖。 */
104	function attackSlot(e: Enemy, table: ReadonlyArray<readonly [number, number]>): { atk: number; inSlot: number; dur: number } {
105	  let slot = 0, consumed = 0;
106	  while (slot < table.length) {
107	    const dur = table[slot][1];
108	    if (dur + consumed > e.ai1) break;
109	    consumed += dur;
110	    slot++;
111	  }
112	  if (slot === table.length) { slot = 0; e.ai1 = 0; consumed = 0; }
113	  const atk = table[slot][0];
114	  return { atk, inSlot: e.ai1 - consumed, dur: table[slot][1] };
115	}
116	
117	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
118	// AI_069 猪鲨鱼龙(NPC 370,NPC.cs:49143-50127)
119	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
120	// 状态 ai0:-1 出生(75t) → 一阶段循环 0 悬浮(num3 t)→1 冲撞(num6 t)×5→2 泡泡弹(80t)
121	//   →3 鲨鱼龙卷(90t);血 ≤50% →4 变身(180t) →5 二阶段循环:0→6 冲撞→7 旋转泡泡冲刺(120t)
122	//   →8 鲨鱼龙卷;9(expert 15% 血)二段变身(180t) →10 隐身悬浮→11 冲撞/12 瞬移(30t)/13 旋转冲刺。
123	// 经典参数:悬浮 60t/0.45/7.5、冲撞 30t@16 速、泡泡 80t 每 4t 一发、龙卷 90t(60t 时发射)、变身 180t。
124	
125	const DUKE = {
126	  spawnT: 75,          // num21 :49215
127	  hoverT: 60,          // num3  :49168(经典)
128	  hoverAcc: 0.45,      // num4  :49169
129	  hoverSpd: 7.5,       // num5  :49170
130	  chargeT: 30,         // num6  :49187
131	  chargeSpd: 16,       // num7  :49188
132	  bubbleT: 80,         // num8  :49202
133	  bubbleEvery: 4,      // num9  :49203
134	  bubbleAcc: 0.3,      // num10 :49204
135	  bubbleSpd: 5,        // num11 :49205
136	  nadoT: 90,           // num12 :49206
137	  p1T: 180,            // num13 :49207(一→二阶段变身)
138	  p2T: 180,            // num14 :49208(二段狂暴变身,expert)
139	  teleT: 30,           // num15 :49209(瞬移)
140	  spinT: 120,          // num16 :49210(旋转冲刺)
141	  spinEvery: 4,        // num17 :49211
142	  spinBubbleSpd: 6,    // num18 :49212
143	  spinDashSpd: 20,     // num19 :49213
144	};
145	/** 旋转冲刺角速度 num20 = 2π/(num16/2) = 2π/60(:49214) */
146	const DUKE_SPIN_RATE = TAU / (DUKE.spinT / 2);
147	
148	/** 狂暴判定 flag6(:49238):玩家在太空(y<800)/地下(y>worldSurface*16)/离开两侧海洋带 */
149	function dukeEnraged(game: GameHooks, player: Player): boolean {
150	  const st = game.world.store;
151	  const surface = (game.world.groundLevel || Math.floor(st.h * 0.3)) * TILE;
152	  return player.y < 800 || player.y > surface ||
153	    (player.x > 6400 && player.x < st.w * TILE - 6400);
154	}
155	
156	/** 一阶段悬浮结束的下一招(:49471-49542) */
157	function dukePick1(e: Enemy, enraged: boolean): number {
158	  const a3 = Math.floor(e.ai3);
159	  let next = 0; // 0=无事(原版 switch 无匹配分支,ai2 继续累计)
160	  if (a3 >= 0 && a3 <= 9) next = 1;          // 冲撞
161	  else if (a3 === 10) { e.ai3 = 1; next = 2; } // 泡泡弹
162	  else if (a3 === 11) { e.ai3 = 0; next = 3; } // 鲨鱼龙卷
163	  if (enraged && next === 2) next = 3;       // :49495 狂暴时泡泡弹换龙卷
164	  if (e.hp <= e.maxHp * 0.5) next = 4;       // :49499 半血变身
165	  return next;
166	}
167	/** 二阶段悬浮结束的下一招(:49736-49809) */
168	function dukePick2(e: Enemy, enraged: boolean): number {
169	  const a3 = Math.floor(e.ai3);
170	  let next = 0;
171	  if (a3 >= 0 && a3 <= 5) next = 1;          // 冲撞(6)
172	  else if (a3 === 6) { e.ai3 = 1; next = 2; } // 旋转泡泡冲刺(7)
173	  else if (a3 === 7) { e.ai3 = 0; next = 3; } // 鲨鱼龙卷(8)
174	  // flag2(expert hp≤15%):next=4 → ai0=9(:49756-49759)——经典模式不可达,代码保留见 dukeState9
175	  if (enraged && next === 2) next = 3;       // :49760
176	  return next;
177	}
178	/** 冲撞起手(:49503-49520 / :49764-49781 / :49988-50004 同构):定速直冲+朝向修正 */
179	function dukeCharge(e: Enemy, player: Player, spd: number, toState: number, st: DukeX) {
180	  const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
181	  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
182	  e.vx = (dx / d) * spd;
183	  e.vy = (dy / d) * spd;
184	  e.ai0 = toState; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0;
185	  e.spin = Math.atan2(e.vy, e.vx);
186	  const s = Math.sign(dx) || st.dir;
187	  st.dir = s;
188	  if (st.spr === 1) e.spin += Math.PI;
189	  st.spr = -s;
190	}
191	/** 悬浮段共用(:49415-49468 / :49680-49733 / :49932-49967 同构):
192	 *  目标点 = 玩家 + (ai1 侧偏, -200);desired = normalize(目标-自身-速度)*spd 后 SimpleFlyMovement */
193	function dukeHover(e: Enemy, player: Player, acc: number, spd: number, sideOff: number, st: DukeX) {
194	  if (e.ai1 === 0) e.ai1 = sideOff * Math.sign(e.cx - player.cx || 1);
195	  const dx = player.cx + e.ai1 - e.cx - e.vx;
196	  const dy = player.cy - 200 - e.cy - e.vy;
197	  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
198	  e.vx = accelTo(e.vx, (dx / d) * spd, acc);
199	  e.vy = accelTo(e.vy, (dy / d) * spd, acc);
200	  const s = Math.sign(player.cx - e.cx) || st.dir;
201	  if (e.ai2 === 0 && s !== st.dir) e.spin += Math.PI;
202	  st.dir = s;
203	  if (st.spr !== -st.dir) e.spin += Math.PI;
204	  st.spr = -st.dir;
205	}
206	
207	/** AI_069 主入口(经典模式) */
208	export function dukeFishronAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
209	  const st = dukeOf(e);
210	  if (!e.bInit) { e.bInit = true; e.ai0 = -1; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; e.spawnAlpha = 255; } // :49247-49257
211	  const b = baseOf(e);
212	  const enraged = player ? dukeEnraged(game, player) : false;
213	  const flag = e.hp <= e.maxHp * 0.5;                       // flag  :49147 二阶段
214	  const flag3 = e.ai0 > 4;                                  // flag3 :49149(二阶段状态 5-13)
215	  const flag4 = e.ai0 > 9;                                  // flag4 :49150(expert 狂暴段 10-13)
216	  const flag5 = e.ai3 < (flag3 ? 3 : 5) * 2;                // flag5 :49151-49152
217	
218	  // 伤害/防御修正(:49153-49167 + 狂暴 :49239-49245)——每 tick 从基值派生
219	  if (flag4) { e.def.damage = Math.round(b.dmg * 1.1); e.def.defense = 0; }
220	  else if (flag3) { e.def.damage = Math.round(b.dmg * 1.2); e.def.defense = Math.round(b.def * 0.8); }
221	  else { e.def.damage = b.dmg; e.def.defense = b.def; }
222	  if (enraged) { e.def.damage = b.dmg * 2; e.def.defense = b.def * 2; }
223	
224	  // 悬浮/冲撞参数(:49168-49201;expert 档注释)
225	  let hoverT = DUKE.hoverT, hoverAcc = DUKE.hoverAcc, hoverSpd = DUKE.hoverSpd;
226	  if (flag4) { hoverAcc = 0.7; hoverSpd = 12; hoverT = 30; }            // :49171-49176
227	  else if (flag3 && flag5) { hoverAcc = 0.5; hoverSpd = 8; hoverT = 20; } // :49177-49182
228	  else if (flag5 && !flag3 && !flag4) hoverT = 30;                       // :49183-49186
229	  let chargeT = DUKE.chargeT, chargeSpd = DUKE.chargeSpd;
230	  if (flag4) { chargeT = 25; chargeSpd = 27; }                           // :49189-49193(expert 同档)
231	  if (enraged) { hoverT = 10; chargeSpd += 6; }                          // :49239-49245
232	
233	  // 玩家死/远离 5600px(:49218-49237):减速升空离场(原版另 EncourageDespawn(10);
234	  // 本工程 Boss 不参与 despawn 块,故仅保留升空)。原版此处无回血逻辑。
235	  if (!player || player.dead || Math.hypot(player.cx - e.cx, player.cy - e.cy) > 5600) {
236	    e.vy -= 0.4;
237	    e.encourageDespawn(10); // :49234 急升逃离 + EncourageDespawn(10)
238	    e.ai0 = e.ai0 > 4 ? 5 : 0;
239	    e.ai2 = 0;
```

```
406	    case 10: { // expert 狂暴段悬浮(:49932-50018)
407	      if (player.dead) break;

409	      dukeHover(e, player, hoverAcc, hoverSpd, 360, st);
410	      e.ai2 += 1;
411	      if (e.ai2 >= hoverT) {
412	        const a3 = Math.floor(e.ai3);
413	        const next = (a3 === 0 || a3 === 2 || a3 === 3 || a3 === 5 || a3 === 6 || a3 === 7) ? 1 : 2;
414	        if (next === 1) dukeCharge(e, player, chargeSpd, 11, st);
415	        else { e.ai0 = 12; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; }
416	      }
417	      break;
418	    }
419	    case 11: { // expert 狂暴段冲撞(:50019-50047)
420	      e.spawnAlpha = Math.max(0, e.spawnAlpha - 25);
421	      game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#8FD8FF', 2, 1.5, { life: 18 });
422	      e.ai2 += 1;
423	      if (e.ai2 >= chargeT) { e.ai0 = 10; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 += 1; }
424	      break;
425	    }
426	    case 12: { // expert 瞬移爆冲(:50048-50107)
427	      invuln = true;
428	      e.spawnAlpha = Math.min(255, e.spawnAlpha + 17);
429	      e.vx *= 0.98;
430	      e.vy = lerp(e.vy, 0, 0.02);
431	      if (e.ai2 === DUKE.teleT / 2) { // :50066-50093 中点瞬移到玩家另一侧
432	        if (e.ai1 === 0) e.ai1 = 300 * Math.sign(e.cx - player.cx || 1);
433	        e.x = player.cx - e.ai1 - e.w / 2;
434	        e.y = player.cy - 200 - e.h / 2;
435	        // :50075-50091 侧翻补偿:瞬移后 num41=玩家在新身哪侧,direction 重定;
436	        // spriteDirection != -direction 时 rotation += π(朝向翻转 180°,
437	        // 2026-08-19 台账 E 补——曾漏 = 瞬移后鱼头朝向不变);(原版 :50078 的
438	        // ai[2]==0 门在 ai[2]==num15/2 内恒假=死代码,略)
439	        const s = Math.sign(player.cx - e.cx);
440	        if (s !== 0) {
441	          st.dir = s;
442	          if (st.spr !== -s) e.spin += Math.PI;
443	          st.spr = -s;
444	        }
445	        game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#8FD8FF', 14, 2.4, { life: 30 });
446	        game.playSfxWav?.('Zombie_20', 1, e.cx, e.cy);   // :50064 PlaySound(29,…,20)(曾借 roar 0.6)
447	      }
448	      e.ai2 += 1;
449	      if (e.ai2 >= DUKE.teleT) {
450	        e.ai0 = 10; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 += 1;
451	        if (e.ai3 >= 9) e.ai3 = 0;
452	      }
453	      break;
454	    }
455	    case 13: { // expert 狂暴段旋转冲刺(:50108-50125;不吐泡泡)
456	      if (e.ai2 === 0) game.playSfxWav?.('Zombie_20', 1, e.cx, e.cy);   // :50112 PlaySound(29,…,20)(曾借 roar 0.6)
457	      {
458	        const r = -DUKE_SPIN_RATE * st.dir;
459	        const cos = Math.cos(r), sin = Math.sin(r);
460	        const nvx = e.vx * cos - e.vy * sin, nvy = e.vx * sin + e.vy * cos;
461	        e.vx = nvx; e.vy = nvy;
462	        e.spin -= DUKE_SPIN_RATE * st.dir;
463	      }
464	      e.ai2 += 1;
465	      if (e.ai2 >= DUKE.spinT) { e.ai0 = 10; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 += 1; }
466	      break;
467	    }
468	    default: break;
469	  }
470	  if (invuln) e.iframes = 2;
471	  e.facing = st.spr;
472	  e.x += e.vx;
473	  e.y += e.vy;
474	}
475	
476	/** 鲨鱼龙卷(状态 3 :49638-49660 / 状态 8 :49865-49885 共用):
477	 *  减速悬停,ai2==60(nadoT-30)时发射龙卷水弹 proj 385(伤害 0 的落地生成器)——
478	 *  小龙卷两发斜落(±dir*2, 8)、克苏龙卷一发原地(0,0,ai0=1=追踪型,ai2=狂暴旗)。
479	 *  落地/近身 50px 后由弹体自身 Kill 生成 384/386 龙卷,龙卷逐段向上生长并周期
480	 *  NewNPC 372/373 鲨鱼(见 DukeSharknadoBolt / DukeSharknadoTornado)。 */
481	function dukeSharknado(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player, st: DukeX, big: boolean) {
482	  e.vx *= 0.98;
483	  e.vy = lerp(e.vy, 0, 0.02);
484	  if (e.ai2 === DUKE.nadoT - 30) {
485	    if (big) {
486	      // :49871 大龙卷 = PlaySound(29,…,20) Zombie_20 满响(曾借 roar 0.7)
487	      game.playSfxWav?.('Zombie_20', 1, e.cx, e.cy);
488	      // 状态 8:克苏龙卷弹(:49873-49876;ai2=flag6 狂暴→追踪速度 +12)
489	      addProj(game, new DukeSharknadoBolt(e.cx, e.cy, 0, 0, true, dukeEnraged(game, player), e));
490	    } else {
491	      // :49644 小龙卷 = PlaySound(29,…,9) Zombie_9 满响
492	      game.playSfxWav?.('Zombie_9', 1, e.cx, e.cy);
493	      // 状态 3:左右两发斜落(:49646-49651;vector8 = rotation·(dir,0)·(w+20)/2 偏移)
494	      const ox = Math.cos(e.spin) * st.dir * (e.w + 20) / 2;
495	      addProj(game, new DukeSharknadoBolt(e.cx + ox, e.cy, st.dir * 2, 8, false, false, e));
496	      addProj(game, new DukeSharknadoBolt(e.cx + ox, e.cy, -st.dir * 2, 8, false, false, e));
497	    }
498	  }
499	  e.ai2 += 1;
500	  if (e.ai2 >= DUKE.nadoT) { e.ai0 = big ? 5 : 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; }
501	}
502	
503	/** 投射桶入桶辅助(同 bossAI.addProj;类在本文件内,避免跨模块构造注入) */
504	function addProj(game: GameHooks, p: Entity): void {
505	  (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (e: Entity, b: string) => void } }).entities.add(p, 'projectiles');
506	}
507	
508	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

```

```json
	{
```

```
	public NPC FindTargetWithinRange(float maxRange, bool checkCanHit = false)
	{
		NPC result = null;
		float num = maxRange;
		for (int i = 0; i < Main.maxNPCs; i++)
		{
			NPC nPC = Main.npc[i];
			if (nPC.CanBeChasedBy(this) && localNPCImmunity[i] == 0 && (!checkCanHit || Collision.CanHitLine(position, width, height, nPC.position, nPC.width, nPC.height)))
			{
				float num2 = Distance(nPC.Center);
				if (!(num <= num2))
				{
					num = num2;
					result = nPC;
				}
			}
		}
		return result;
	}
```

```
export function shoot(game: GameHooks, x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, dmg: number, projId: number, life = 300, arrowOpts?: Partial<import('./Arrow').ArrowOpts>): void {
  (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (e: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities.add(
    new Arrow(x, y, vx, vy, dmg, 0, projId, null, { grav: 0, life, pierce: 1, hostile: true, ...arrowOpts }), 'projectiles');
}
```

```
/** Main.projFrames（Main.cs:8392 起全 275 条非 1 帧赋值，tools 内联提取）：
 *  未列入的恒 1 帧。投射物贴图是【竖向多帧行】——帧高 = 图高/帧数，
 *  整图绘制会把多帧压成胶片条（史莱姆随从 266 曾 12 帧压成一坨） */
import projFramesJson from '../data/vanilla-projframes.json';
import { projectileData } from '../data/vanillaProjectiles';
import { projGravSpec } from '../data/vanillaItemCombat';
const PROJ_FRAMES = projFramesJson as Record<string, number>;
export function projFrameCount(projId: number): number {
  return PROJ_FRAMES[String(projId)] ?? 1;
}

/** 单帧裁切缓存（id+帧号 → canvas），多帧行按帧高切片 */
const frameCache = new Map<string, HTMLCanvasElement>();
/** 热补丁替换弹幕贴图(2026-08-19 素材重制):写 spriteCache + 清该 id 的
 *  frameCache 条目(键 `id|idx` 不含 texId,不自动失效)。RemasterRuntime 调用。 */
export function setProjSpriteOverride(projId: number, img: ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement): void {
  spriteCache.set(projId, img);
  const prefix = `${projId}|`;
  for (const k of frameCache.keys()) if (k.startsWith(prefix)) frameCache.delete(k);
}
export function projFrameImg(projId: number, frameIdx: number): HTMLCanvasElement | null {
  const img = projSprite(projId);
  if (!img || !(img.width > 0) || img.width === 0) return null;
  const frames = projFrameCount(projId);
  const idx = Math.max(0, Math.min(frames - 1, frameIdx));
  const fh = img.height / frames;
  if (!Number.isFinite(fh) || fh < 1) return null;
  const key = `${projId}|${idx}`;
  let c = frameCache.get(key);
  if (c) return c;
  c = document.createElement('canvas');
  c.width = img.width;
  c.height = Math.round(fh);
  const cx = c.getContext('2d')!;
  cx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
  cx.drawImage(img, 0, Math.round(idx * fh), img.width, Math.round(fh), 0, 0, c.width, c.height);
  if (frameCache.size > 2048) frameCache.clear();
  frameCache.set(key, c);
  return c;
}

export interface ArrowOpts {
  /** 重力/tick（aiStyle1/2 = 0.3；直飞魔法弹传 0）。默认 0.3 */
```
